研 究  報 告                
聯發科(2454.TT) 
產業：電子–半導體 
再次上調 AI ASIC 貢獻，自 4Q26 起展現優異成⻑動能  
 投資評價與建議 
AI ASIC業務進展更順利，再次上調自4Q26年起營收貢獻，將是未來公司營運
成⻑主軸，2027年起將明顯展現優異成⻑動能。 
 重點評論分析 
3Q26營收及毛利率財測皆符合預期 ：手機營收將季持平或下降中個位數百分
比，智慧裝置平台營收季增中高個位數百分比，PMIC季持平，財測為營收
1,522~1,598億美元，毛利率46%±1.5%，費用率31%±2%，符合預期。 
再次上調AI ASIC預估值，未來展望正向：1) AI ASIC專案進展順利，4Q26營
收貢獻提升至超越20億美元，並於2027年加速成⻑，並預估2027年市占率為
800億美元之15~20%，第二個ASIC專案進展順利，預計在2028年量產，市占
率也會更高，也持續深耕與多家客戶；2)展望未來，智慧邊緣平台受惠連網、
運算需求和車用產品市占率提升，及部分TV SOC因反映成本而提高ASP，展
望正向，而AI ASIC貢獻更上調至超越20億美元，隨著供應鏈成本全面上升，
正採取必要的價格調整措施，毛利率有望維持在46%水準，整體而言，公司認
為2026年營收將年增高個位數百分比，較上次法說的年增中高個位數百分比
更正向，且AI ASIC業務更樂觀亦讓公司對成⻑性更有把握。 
 投資風險 
CSP客戶生產時程遞延。

### Page 2

| 營運概況  
2Q26營收超越財測上緣，獲利優於預期：1.2Q26營收1,521.8億元 ， 超越財測1,402~1,492億元上緣 ， QoQ+2% ，
主要來自智慧裝置平台業務穩健的成⻑動能，毛利率46.2%，符合財測，EPS 15.28元，優於預期。 
3Q26營收及毛利率財測皆符合預期：公司維持手機2026年出貨年減15%之預估，客戶聚焦於旗艦機種與入門機
種，而旗艦機SOC放量有望抵銷其他機種的疲弱，手機營收將季持平或下降中個位數百分比，智慧裝置平台營收
季增中高個位數百分比(通訊、運算與車用市占率提升，及反映TV SOC的DRAM漲價)，PMIC與2Q26持平，財測
為營收1,522~1,598億美元，QoQ+0%~5%，毛利率46%±1.5%，費用率31%±2%，財測符合預期，預估3Q26
營收1,590億元，QoQ+4.5%，毛利率46.4%， EPS 14.55元。 
再次上調AI ASIC預估值，未來展望正向：本次法說會焦點多數落在AI ASIC業務，1)AI ASIC專案進展順利，4Q26
營收貢獻提升至超越20億美元(前次法說為20億美元)，並於2027年加速成⻑，預估2027年AI ASIC SAM約800億
美元(前次為700~800億美元，且僅針對ASIC，不包含HBM)，市占率提升至15~20%(前次市占率為10~15%)，
2028年將遠高於2027年，第二個ASIC專案進展順利，預計在2028年量產，市占率也會更高，也持續深耕與多家
客戶，且EMIB-T在良率提升和技術成熟度方面都取得了良好的進展，有望在2028年實現量產；2)每⼀代ASIC毛利
率都相近，會略稀釋目前公司整體毛利率，但營業利益率將提顯著提升；3)在448G SerDes的開發上進展順利，透
過CPO系統解決方案，提供業界頂尖的效能與功耗表現，也持續在台積電(2330)的COUPE平台上開發CPO解決方
案，以實現次世代的系統連接能力；4)機架設計已取得進展，擁有自己IT系統包含SerDes D2D和3.5D封裝，並整
合NVLink Fusion。 
展望未來，1)智慧邊緣平台受惠連網、運算需求和車用產品市占率提升，及部分TV SOC因反映成本而提高ASP，
展望正向，而AI ASIC貢獻更上調至超越20億美元，隨著供應鏈成本全面上升，正採取必要的價格調整措施，毛利
率有望維持在46%水準 ； 2)雖手機業務持續保守 ， 然2nm SOC於3Q26推出 ， 有望彌補其他機種的疲弱 ， 預期2027
年旗艦機市占率進⼀步提升，整體而言，公司認為2026年營收將年增高個位數百分比，較上次法說的年增中高個
位數百分比更正向，顯見手機業務已是谷底，且AI ASIC業務更樂觀亦讓公司對成⻑性更有把握。 
AI業務展望更正向，2027年起營運將顯著展現優異成⻑動能，調升至買進評等：AI ASIC業務進展順利，再次上調
自4Q26年起營收貢獻，將是未來公司營運成⻑主軸，2027年起將明顯展現優異成⻑動能，投資評等調升至買進，
給予40倍PER*2027年EPS評價。

### Page 3

Unimicron (3037.TW)
30 July 2026 Citi Research
3
EMIB-T under development; optimism about L-T GM; glass substrate still far
away — For EMIB-T, Unimicron and another two Japanese peers are developing
this technology with yield rate target of 50% reached at the first phase. Mass
production timing would be in 2027. Despite investors’ concerns over poor GM
profile for EMIB-T on low yield rate, the company indicated there are guaranteed
minimum GM profiles on low yield. And if higher yield rate could be met, there

would be extra bonus awarded based on the incentive program. The mgmt. also
indicated that she thinks 50-60% GM profile for ABF substrate is a reasonable
range in the mid to long term in this cycle. For glass substrate, the company is
working with multiple customers and expects mass production would not occur
until 2030.
Earnings adjustment; reiterate our Buy rating — We adjust our 2026/27 /28E
earnings to factor in 1) depreciation from Thailand plant and 2) dilution impact from

its recent fundraising. We remain constructive on the ABF sector and reiterate our
Buy rating with unchanged DCF-based TP NT$1,500 (implied 31x 2027E EPS).
Figure 1. Unimicron's 2Q26 results

### Page 7

Unimicron (3037.TW)
30 July 2026 Citi Research
7
Unimicron
Company description
Established in 1990, Unimicron is the world's No.1 PCB maker and HDI board
maker. After the acquisition of PPT in late 2009, its revenue exposure to
substrate has surpassed 40%, with the rest almost equally shared by
conventional PCB and HDI. Unimicron has manufacturing sites in Taiwan and
China (Suzhou, Kunshan, Shenzhen). UMC is the largest shareholder, with a
~13% stake.

Investment strategy
We rate Unimicron shares a Buy. Our positive view is premised on: more
upside potential in the ABF segment thanks to product-mix improvements,
better pricing on more complex product upgrade, and capacity expansion.
Despite macro uncertainty, from a long-term perspective, we believe the
tightness in ABF is unlikely to ease without much new capacity ramped
aggressively in the upcoming years, and Unimicron should be the largest
beneficiary with its scale in ABF. Thanks to AI GPU/ASIC demand, we now see

strong growth in 2026/27E ABF consumption. We believe Unimicron could
see strong revenue growth again as it did in 2019–21 from both ABF new
capacity and a BT rebound.
Valuation
Our target price for Unimicron is NT$1,500. We use a 1-year forward-looking
DCF-based valuation (ie, end-2027) to reflect Unimicron's ability to generate
stable cash. With a risk-free rate of 4.3% (10-year government bond rate), a
market risk premium of 7%, and an equity beta of 1.43 (2-year weekly share

price), we calculate Unimicron's WACC as 13.7%. Our target price is equivalent
to 66x/31x 2026/27E EPS, which is higher than the +1std level of consensus
PE multiple of the past five years. However, as the market focuses on 2027
upcycle, we believe valuation on 2027E EPS is still in line with historical peak
valuations of 25–30x during the last upcycles.
Risks
Key downside risks that could mean Unimicron shares trade below our target
price include: 1) weaker-than-expected HDI business; 2) significant increase

in ABF supply in the industry; and 3) stronger-than-expected margin pressure
from depreciation due to higher capex investments.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment
rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered

stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating
and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also

suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price
chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in

exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the
expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility

or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:
Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside

call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is
moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day

period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be
different from and does not affect a stock’s fundamental equity rating, which reflects a longer-term total absolute return

## Document 082: 260729_daiwa_unimicron

Source PDF: all report pdf/260729_daiwa_unimicron.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-07-29
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We provide our latest view on Unimicron after 2Q26 results.

What’s the impact: 2Q26 results beat. Unimicron’s 2Q26 earnings came 
in at TWD13.1bn (EPS: TWD8.53), which was 196% and 141% higher than 
our and the street’s expectations due to a higher-than-expected gross 
margin and non-operating gains. Unimicron’s 2Q26 revenue grew 15% 
QoQ to TWD42.9bn, while we attribute revenue strength to price hikes on 
substrate and new capacity ramp. Management attributes half of the 
margin expansion in 2Q26 to price hikes and the rest to utilisation rate 
(UTR) and yield improvement. As such, gross margin expanded 6.9pp QoQ 
to 24.8%; operating margin thus expanded by 8.2pp QoQ on gross margin 
expansion and better opex control. Additionally, Unimicron recognised a 
mark-to-market gain of TWD9.2bn mainly from its holding in UMC in 2Q26.

Business outlook. We believe management holds a positive view on both 
revenue and gross margin trends in 2H26 given the rising AI substrate and 
PCB sales mix and ongoing cost inflation. In the results call, we noted 
investors are keen to know the development of EMIB-T and glass-core 
substrate, capacity expansion plan, margin trend/pricing trend, HDI 
strategy, etc. For EMIB-T, management guides that end customers will 
likely adopt this technology for at least three generations in their products, 
which would lead to Unimicron and the other two JP substrate companies 
investing in related equipment in this new packaging technology and target 
the first stage of yield rate at 50% in 2H27E. Of total capex, management 
believes only 1-2% is tailored for EMIB-T equipment, while most of the rest 
(c.85%) is still marked for ABF substrate capacity expansion. As Unimicron 
further raised capex guidance to TWD53.7bn, we expect its ABF capacity 
expansion plan to reach 40%/20%/20% in 2026/27/28E respectively. 
Unimicron may also drive slightly incremental capacity from de-
bottlenecking. As for HDI strategy, it mentioned no direct contest with its 
Chinese competitor and a focus on non-China markets. Considering the 
enlarging substate body size due to die size or co-packaging requirements 
and advanced technology has to cooperate with substrate, mgmt. expects 
value from substrate to gradually increase with long-term GM target at 50-
60%. As such, we expect GM to expand to 26.3/38.6/45.4% (up 3-7pp vs. 
our prior estimate) in 2026/27/28, respectively, along with price hike trend.

What we recommend: We lift our 2026-28E EPS by 31-66% due to higher 
gross margin assumptions. We reaffirm our Buy (1) call and raise our 12M 
TP to TWD1,500 on an unchanged target PER of 35x applied to 1-year-
forward EPS. Downside risk: weaker-than-expected PC/server demand.

How we differ: Our 2026-28E EPS are 26-33% above the Bloomberg 
consensus, likely due to our more bullish gross margin assumptions.

29 July 2026

2Q26 results: on-track gross margin expansion

➢ Gross margin further expands on price hikes and technology migration 
➢ Strong capex guidance indicates a decent ABF substrate outlook  
➢ Reiterating Buy (1) call; lifting 12M TP to TWD1,500 (from TWD1,000)

Company profile 
Unimicron is a leading global manufacturer of IC substrate. The company also produces high-density 
interconnection (HDI) boards, flexible PCBs (FPC), and rigid flex PCBs (RFPCB). The company has 
manufacturing sites in Taiwan, China, Japan and Germany, with products' major applications including 
servers, PC/NB, and smartphones.

### Page 1

M
Update
Realtek Semiconductor  | Asia Pacific
Better than feared outlook
The stock appears to be a safe harbor vs. other high-flyers (e.g.,
Memory) following the trough into 2Q earnings .
2Q review: 2Q revenue was NT$37.6bn, +3% Q/Q and +18% Y/Y, 3% above MSe. 2Q
gross profit was NT$17.7bn. GM was 47.0%, -2.7ppt Q/Q and -3.2ppt Y/Y, 2.7ppt
below MSe. EPS was NT$7.42, -12% Q/Q and -3% Y/Y, 12% below MSe. Margin
compression was driven by elevated memory pricing and constrained OSAT capacity.

2H26 outlook: PC segment faces demand pressure in 2H26 due to elevated memory
costs and CPU supply tightness, but revenue growth is expected, helped by spec-up
demand. For the consumer segment, TV shipments have begun to soften after FIFA;
home appliance demand continues to grow steadily and global game console
shipments are expected to fall this year. The networking segment demonstrated
demand stability, supported by enterprise infra refresh cycles and the transition

toward higher-value WiFi7 and multi-gig Ethernet solutions. The auto segment is
expected to be seasonally strong in 2H, especially helped by growth from the
European market.
Other highlights: Increased product pricing with certain customers is to take effect
in 3Q, helping GM recover. Realtek is gaining share in the managed switch market,
having increased 2-3ppt vs the previous reporting period. WiFi7 penetration in
notebooks is estimated to reach ~30% this year, while WiFi8 deployment is seen

starting from 1Q28.
Remain EW: The company is indeed showing better than feared outlook due to
FOMP (link). We believe the stock is fairly valued relative to our implied target of
19x 2026E P/E. Supply constraints and weak end-market demand continues, but we
expect the company to manage some revenue growth in 3Q and a continued GM
rebound in 2H, helped by spec-up demand and price increases.
Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+
Daniel Yen, CFA
Equity Analyst

Valuation methodology

assumptions for cost of equity of 9.6%, intermediate growth rate of 7.3%, payout ratio of

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

ab 30 July 2026
Global Research
Delta Electronics
Outlook intact with HVDC shipping from Q4
Q2 results in-line with latest expectations and milder due to supply constraints
Q226 results were in-line with sales at NT$183.3bn +15% QoQ/+48% YoY driven by
cooling component rush orders driving +22% QoQ/+85% YoY growth to  infrastructure
(34%) while segment OpM  was -180bp to 17.9%. Power electronics (53%) was +13%
QoQ/+48% YoY to NT96.3bn, with OPM of  20.9%. Automation NT$14.3bn, +8% QoQ

/ +11% YoY but reversed to op loss of NT$443mn due to memory inflation, while
mobility NT$8.2bn, +3% QoQ/-26% YoY with NT$744mn op loss. Blended GMs/OpM
declined 140bp/110bp QoQ to 35.6%/16.7% less Q1 benefits from delivered duty on
liquid-cooling products and1x reversals, but booked NT$4.4bn non-op gain and FX to
lift EPS to NT$9.68 above prior UBS/Street NT$9.36/NT$9.58. The margin impact was
less than Delta Thailand as the factory network absorbed component costs/inventory

reserves vs. the parent seeing higher margin system and solution sales.
H226 strong outlook intact, with margins gradually improving
We maintain Q326 +15% QoQ led by GB power supply while liquid cooling could be
slightly weaker given the high base in Q2. We model GMs mildly rebounding from
35.6% to 36.1%/36.7% in Q3/Q4 as the company qualifies 2nd sources and negotiates
to pass through higher costs.  Mix shift to server power supply also should support some

mild margin recovery.  The company stays aggressive on investments, targeting opex
growth of +30% YoY to support heavier system design as power architectures grow
more complex moving toward HVDC/SST and adds more US field support. It also expects
AI demand to sustain next couple years, with capex rising from NT$46.5bn to NT
$67.5bn to expand in Taiwan, Thailand and China for AI and fuel cell investment.
Power racks start initial shipments in Q4 and ramp into ASICs in 2027

The HVDC power racks will start MP in 3Q with small shipments starting  4Q mainly on
+/-400V for ASIC pushing aggressively for next year up to ⅓ of mix but supply
constrained by tight supercapacitors.  The company expects milder ramp for NVIDIA's
800VDC as customers are staying more conservative on adoption. We view the milder
roadmap on Rubin and Rubin ultra staying with the Oberon NVL72 keeps that option
optional for the next 1-2 years. Amid mild HVDC initial ramp, it still stays constructive on

its strong pipeline and push for data center deliveries through 2027 to resolve tight
compute demand even amid the concerns on hyperscaler cash flows.
Estimates intact - Maintain Buy and NT$2,750
With results and outlook in-line with preview, we fine-tune 2026 from NT$43.0 to NT
$42.3 and maintain 2027/28 EPS at NT$67.5/NT$90.  Delta's stock has pulled back 36%
since May due to Kyber delays / less aggressive HVDC ramp, milder Q2 sales / margin
performance and broader AI correction,  pulling it back to 22x/17x 2026-27 EPS.   We

now see better entry with sales ramping in H226, spec upgrades to ASIC power racks
and Rubin at 60% higher TDP over Blackwell and margins holding better than feared.

Company Description
Delta Electronics provides computer switching power supply solutions, as well as video display
and electronics components, for computers, smartphones, telecom and industrial products.
Founded in 1971, the firm has production bases in Taiwan, China, Thailand and India. In

2023, its power electronics, infrastructure and automation segments contributed 62%, 25%
and 13% of revenue, respectively.
UBS EPS and Consensus EPS
The UBS EPS is an adjusted diluted EPS metric. It is calculated using the UBS analyst's
interpretation of earnings suitable for valuation purposes divided by the diluted number of
shares. This may differ to the way the consensus EPS metric has been calculated.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We base our price target on PE multiple valuation.

Delta's businesses include SPS and components for a variety of end-applications, video
displays and data networking subsystem and system products. Any changes in these end-
markets could affect our forecasts for Delta. Investment risk could increase/decrease on any
deviation from our expectations for end-market demand and competition.
Downside risks for Delta include: severe supply chain constraints; weaker-than-expected PC/
server demand; a slower-than-expected IA recovery; and a slower ramp of 5G in 2022.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.
Important Information About Sustainable Investing Strategies: Sustainable investing strategies aim to consider and incorporate environmental, social and governance
(ESG) factors into investment process and portfolio construction. Strategies across geographies approach ESG analysis and incorporate the findings in a variety of ways.
Incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing considerations may inhibit the portfolio manager’s ability to participate in certain investment opportunities that otherwise

would be consistent with its investment objective and other principal investment strategies. The returns on a portfolio incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing
considerations may be lower or higher than portfolios where ESG factors, exclusions, or other sustainability issues are not considered by the portfolio manager, and the
investment opportunities available to such portfolios may differ.

## Document 117: 260731_daiwa_Advantech

### Page 1

Summary: Advantech’s 2Q26 net profit was TWD4.52bn (EPS: TWD5.2), 32% higher than 
our estimate (TWD3.42bn) and the Bloomberg consensus (TWD3.43bn). 2Q26 revenue of 
TWD26.1bn was higher than our estimate (TWD22.7bn) and the company’s guidance 
range of TWD20.5-21bn, which we attribute to solid industrial demand and ongoing price 
hikes. Per management, they have seen price hikes proliferating into diversified 
components (such as memory and CPU) This also caused the gross margin (37.7%) in 
2Q26 to be lower than its guidance range of 38-40% and our estimate of 39.8%. However, 
operating margin came in stronger at 19.6% (vs. our estimate of 17.7% and street’s 
estimate of 18.2%) due to better operating leverage despite a lower gross margin.

Looking ahead, while the 2Q26 B/B ratio came down QoQ, management remained positive 
on the revenue momentum in 3Q26. In terms of edge AI, Advantech has seen the 
proliferation into different end applications speeding up since 1Q26. Considering the 
blended gross margin from edge AI is at 40-42%, Advantech’s overall gross margin could 
expand along with a rising edge AI contribution. Advantech will host its 2Q26 results call on 
5 August at 14:00 and we will provide updates in due course. We have an Outperform (2) 
rating on this name.

What’s the impact: 
• 2Q26 results first-take: results beat on pull-in demand. Advantech’s 2Q26 net profit 
was TWD4.52bn (EPS: TWD5.2), 32% higher than our estimate (TWD3.42bn) and the 
Bloomberg consensus (TWD3.43bn). 2Q26 revenue of TWD26.1bn was 15% higher 
than our estimate (TWD22.7bn) and the company’s guidance range of TWD20.5-21bn, 
which we attribute to solid industrial demand and ongoing price hikes. Per management, 
they have seen price hikes proliferating into diversified components (such as memory 
and CPU with price increases of 10-20%). This also caused the gross margin (37.7%) in 
2Q26 to be lower than its guidance range of 38-40% and our estimate of 39.8%. 
However, operating margin came in stronger at 19.6% (vs. our estimate of 17.7% and 
street’s estimate of 18.2%) due to better operating leverage despite a lower gross 
margin. Lastly, Advantech’s B/B ratio decreased to 1.44 in 2Q26 (1Q26: 1.77; 4Q25: 
1.33; 3Q25: 1.01).

• Business outlook. Looking ahead, while the 2Q26 B/B ratio came down QoQ, 
management remained positive on the revenue momentum in 3Q26. As for the gross 
margin, the company is still aggressively reviewing its pricing in order to pass through 
additional material costs. As such, the longer-term gross margin range should remain at 
39-40% when the raw material environment stabilises. As for edge AI, the company is

## Document 120: 260731_daiwa_Makalot

Source PDF: all report pdf/260731_daiwa_Makalot.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-07-31
Pages: 7

### Page 1

Taiwan Consumer Discretionary

What’s new: Makalot released its updated 2Q26 results on 31 July. We 
provide an update on its outlook for 3Q26 and 2026 (see our previous 
memo on 26 May).

What’s the impact: 2Q26 results beat our forecast but missed the 
Bloomberg consensus. Makalot’s 2Q26 revenue of TWD8,166m (-8.2% 
QoQ, +6.2% YoY) was in line with our forecast but slightly missed the 
consensus estimate. Its 2Q26 gross margin was 24.4% (vs. 26.1% in 
1Q26, 22.0% in 2Q25), beating our estimate of 24.0% but was in line with 
the consensus estimate of 24.5%. Its operating margin of 13.3% (vs. 15.5% 
in 1Q26, 12.2% in 2Q25) was higher than our estimate of 12.3% while 
missing the consensus estimate of 14.0%. Therefore, operating profit of 
TWD1,086m (-21.1% QoQ, +15.4% YoY) beat our forecast by 9.3% and 
missed the consensus by 7.9%. Its pre-tax profit of TWD1,072m (-21.0% 
QoQ, +31.0% YoY) beat our estimate by 11.5% and missed the consensus 
estimates by 9.0%. Its net income was TWD814m (-24.1% QoQ, +32.2% 
YoY), 8.2% higher than our forecast but 9.9% lower than the consensus. Its 
2Q26 EPS ended at TWD3.3.

Unchanged 3Q26 outlook. Due to geopolitical uncertainties and structural 
shifts in pricing dynamics, Makalot maintained its 3Q26 guidance: 
shipments to grow by a mid-single digit rate (4-5%) YoY and ASP to decline 
by 3%, with a gross margin target of 24%. For 2026, it expects growth in 
both revenue and net profit. For 2026 shipments, it guides for a low-single 
digit rate of growth YoY with flat ASP YoY and gross margin of 25%. We 
lower our 2026-27E EPS after 2-6% factoring in 2Q26 results with a more 
conservative operating margin assumption. We introduce our 2028 
forecasts.

What we recommend: We maintain our Hold (3) rating but lower our 12-
month TP to TWD232 (from TWD275), now based on a target PER of 15x 
(from 18x), near the average (19x) of its past-3-year PER trading range of 
14-26x on top of its earnings momentum and order uncertainty in an 
inflationary environment and applied to our 1-year-forward EPS forecast. 
However, we believe its dividend yield of 6.8-7.5% over 2026-28E should 
be its share price support. Key upside/downside risks: higher-/fewer-than-
expected orders from key clients, inflation and tariffs.

How we differ: Our 2026-28E EPS are 8-15% below the Bloomberg 
consensus, likely due to our more conservative revenue, gross and 
operating margin expansion assumptions.

31 July 2026

2Q26 review: order uncertainty continues

➢ 2Q26 results beat our forecast but missed the Bloomberg consensus 
➢ 3Q26 guidance was lowered previously on macro uncertainties 
➢ Maintaining our Hold (3) call; lowering 12-month TP to TWD232

Makalot Industrial (Makalot) is a diversified garment manufacturer for brands and retailers globally. Its 
major clients include Gap, Fast Retailing, Kohl's, Target, and Walmart.

## Document 121: 260731_daiwa_mtk UG

Source PDF: all report pdf/260731_daiwa_mtk UG.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-07-31
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: MediaTek (MTK) released upbeat 2Q26 results but downbeat 
3Q26 guidance. Though the cash-cow mobile business looks to be 
weakening on commodity cost inflation, its new AI business appears to be 
running ahead of expectation. We raise our AI accelerator assumption and 
expect MTK’s AI ASIC revenue contribution to overtake Mobile in mid-2027, 
a year earlier than our previous forecast. Aided by the recent reset of equity 
market’s hefty expectation unlocking value for reinvestment, in our view, we 
upgrade our rating 2 notches to Buy (1) with a refreshed TP of TWD4,088.

What’s the impact: Results upbeat but guidance downbeat. MTK 
reported 2Q26 net profit of TWD24bn, 5%/8% above our/consensus 
estimates, thanks to lower income tax and higher top line, which beat 
guidance (TWD140-149bn) and market expectations on better share gain 
in its Smart-edge business, partially offset by lower margins (table on p.2 
for more details). Guided 3Q26 top line missed our previous forecast due to 
weaker-than-expected Mobile business, where management failed to 
realise its previous expectation calling 2Q26 as the trough, though it 
maintains its forecast for this year’s global smartphone shipment to drop 
c.15% YoY. That said, it sees its AI ASIC business as progressing even 
stronger for “substantial” growth into 2027.

Weaker Mobile more than offset by stronger AI. Following its upward 
revision in the previous quarter, MTK at today’s call further lifted its AI 
accelerator (ex-HBM) serviceable addressable market (SAM) to USD80bn 
for 2027 (previous: USD70-80bn), targeting a market share of 15-20% 
(previous: 10-15%), which translates into USD12-16bn potential revenue 
for the company. This suggests its first AI ASIC for a hyperscale customer 
is on track for mass production (MP) in 4Q26, while the second chip for the 
same customer is on track for MP by early 2028, with higher dollar value on 
stronger performance/watt and lower total cost of ownership (TCO). Net of 
the AI strength and mobile weakness, we raise our 2027-28E EPS by 23-
30%, where we now forecast MTK’s AI ASIC revenue to reach USD2.1bn, 
USD15bn, and USD22bn for 2026, 2027 and 2028, respectively.

What we recommend: Given our forecast revisions, we lift our 12M TP for 
MTK to TWD4,088 (previous: TWD2,454), now based on our 4-quarter 
forward PER target of 36x (previous: 27x), justified by our 2025-28E EPS 
CAGR of 36% on 1x PEG. We upgrade our rating 2 notches to Buy (1) with 
key downside risk of any AI accelerator ramps below our expectation.

How we differ: While our 2026-27E EPS are 7-10% above the consensus 
forecasts, we see the street as too aggressive on MTK’s 2028 AI business. 
e

31 July 2026

Upgrading: value unlocked after expectation reset

➢ 2Q26 results upbeat but 3Q26 guidance downbeat 
➢ Mobile irrelevant now given likely strong AI ramp ahead 
➢ Upgrading rating to Buy (1) with higher 12M TP of TWD4,088

Company profile 
Ranked No.5 in the world by 2023 fabless semiconductor revenue, MediaTek (MTK) offers diverse 
products across optical storage, digital home, smartphone, PC, ASIC, automotive and other IoT 
applications. MTK has successfully captured the industry demand cycles from optical disk drives to 
feature phones to smartphones, and now positions itself well as a platform provider to capitalise on the 
next demand cycle of the BigData/AI to help facilitate digital transformation in human lives.

## Document 124: 260731_gs_kinsus

Source PDF: all report pdf/260731_gs_kinsus.pdf

### Page 2

Investment Thesis - Kinsus 
Kinsus is one of the key IC substrates suppliers globally with ~10% ABF/BT substrates 
market share in 2024, with ~18% of total 2025 revenue contributed by its contact lens 
subsidiary, Pegavision (6941.TW, Not Covered). With our view that the ABF substrate 
shortage started from 2Q26 (ABF substrate return to supply demand balance from late 
2025), we believe Kinsus’ performance will continue to improve compared with high-end 
ABF peers considering the company’s increasing market share in the high-end ABF 
substrate market; however, ~36% of the company’s revenue was contributed by BT 
substrates in 2025 and 30% of the company’s ABF revenue is from non-LTA customers, 
which we believe will continue to enjoy a better pricing outlook. As a result, we believe 
Kinsus’ earnings outlook should continue to be strong (~125% 2026-28E CAGR), mainly 
driven by better BT substrate and non-LTA ABF substrate pricing outlook, despite the 
unfavorable demand outlook before 2H26. We believe Kinsus is an experienced 
substrate supplier with a clear capacity expansion plan that can also upgrade its product 
portfolio in the long term. We believe we are at the beginning of an industry upcycle, 
making us positive on Kinsus’ stock performance over the next 12 months.  
Price Target Risks and Methodology - Kinsus 
Valuation methodology: Our 12-month target price of NT$1,120 for Kinsus is based on 
16.5x 2028E P/E, which is +1.2x STDV higher than Kinsus’ past upcycle average P/E 
(14.2x).  
Key downside risks: (1) Slower-than-expected PC demand recovery, (2) 
Slower-than-expected ABF substrates pricing upgrading outlook, (3) 
Slower/Less-than-expected AI server orders from 2H26.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

MediaTek hosted its 2Q26 analyst meeting on 31 July. 3Q26 
guidance came in better than expected, and the company has 
delivered an even more positive message on its AI ASIC business, 
which we believe further reinforces our view that the AI ASIC 
upcycle is just getting started. Management now expects its AI ASIC 
revenue to exceed US$2bn in 2026 (vs. US$2bn previously) and 
raised its 2027 AI ASIC SAM and market share — which now  
implies US$12-$16bn of AI ASIC revenue in 2027 (vs. US$7-$12bn 
previously). Management expressed its confidence in its next-gen AI 
ASIC project, stating that the tapeout is on track, with high 
volume production planned for early 2028.  Packaging and 
substrate yields are also progressing well, which we believe should 
ease investor concerns around execution. Management now expects 
its first AI ASIC project will also contribute in 2028 along with its 
second project, which is expected to enhance the company’s 
market share significantly. With a stronger near- and 
medium-term outlook driven by AI ASIC business, we reiterate our 
Buy rating on MediaTek and tick up our 12m TP to NT$7,000 (25X 
on 2H27-1H28E EPS) from NT$6,800, implying potential upside of 
~97%. 
Higher AI ASIC SAM with higher market share target 
Management now sees its AI ASIC SAM at US$80bn for 2027 (vs. 
US$70-$80bn previously) and raised its targeted market share to 
15%-20% (vs. 10%-15% previously) — implying US$12-$16bn  
potential AI ASIC revenue in 2027. In the near term, the first AI ASIC 
project remains on schedule, with production beginning in 4Q26. 
2026 AI ASIC revenue is now expected to exceed US$2bn (vs. 
US$2bn previously), and revenue is anticipated to scale substantially 
in 2027, driven by increasing customer demand for its favorable cost 
of ownership.  Overall the upbeat AI ASIC guidance reaffirms our 
confidence in MediaTek’s AI ASIC growth outlook, and we slightly 
tick up our 2026E AI ASIC forecast to US$2.2bn (vs. US$2bn

### Page 3

previously), while maintaining our 2027E AI ASIC revenue forecast at US$20.3bn. We
now expect AI ASIC to account for 10%/48% of MediaTek’s revenue in 2026/27E.
Expect significant market share gains in 2028
Management expressed its confidence that, given the strong capability demonstrated by
the first AI ASIC project, its next gen project is on track with market share improving
significantly as the second AI ASIC project ramps — and guided that its first AI ASIC

project will contribute in 2028 alongside its second project, resulting in enhanced
market share. Although management did not provide guidance on the 2028 SAM, it
stated it is confident that the 2028 SAM will be higher than 2027.  Given MediaTek’s
optimistic market share outlook guidance and strong dollar content growth for next-gen
ASIC, we maintain our 2028E ASIC revenue forecast at US$52.5bn, which is expected
to account for 68% of revenue by 2028E.

Next-gen AI ASIC progressing on track
During the meeting, management addressed investor concerns over its
second-generation project in terms of timeline and production yield. On timeline,
management noted that its second ASIC is on track for high-volume production in
early 2028, with design and tapeout schedules progressing on track. On yield,
management stated the yield and reliability of the second AI ASIC project are also on
track, with improving yield at its substrate vendor. In our view, management was more

constructive on the advanced packaging solution relative to the last quarter, and we
believe this should ease investor concerns on potential execution risk on the second
ASIC project.
Gross margin guidance consistent across generations, but we see potential upside
On margins, management maintained that AI ASIC gross margins will be broadly similar
from generation to generation, and slightly dilutive to the corporate average. However,
the business remains operating margin accretive, with the company’s OpEx ratio

expected to decline substantially as revenue and scale become larger into 2027, even as
absolute OpEx rises on continued technology investment. Overall, we see potential
upside to this guidance over time, as MediaTek’s content value increases with each
generation. We believe the second generation chip carries meaningfully higher silicon
content for MediaTek with a larger chip size, deeper design engagement, additional I/O,
and advanced packaging integration - leading to potentially higher gross margin relative

to the first generation.

2Q26 results and 3Q26 guidance recap

3Q26 revenue guidance above GS and Street 
For 3Q26, MediaTek expects revenue to be in a range of NT$152.2-$159.8bn (0% to 5% 
QoQ), gross margin to be within +/- 1.5% of 46%, and OpEx-to-sales to be within +/- 2% 
of 31%. 3Q26 revenue guidance (at the midpoint) came in higher vs. GSe/Bloomberg 
consensus of NT$153.9bn/NT$152.0bn on stronger smart edge expectations. GM 
guidance of 46% +/1.5% was in line vs. GSe/cons. of 46.1%/46.2%. We are now 
modeling 3Q26E revenue to increase by 4.8% QoQ, with GM of 46.3%. By segment, 
management expects flagship smartphone revenue to be flat to down mid-single digit 
QoQ, with flagship SoC launches largely offsetting weakness in other segment. In the 
smart edge segment, MediaTek is looking for mid-to-high single digit growth QoQ, 
driven by new connectivity/auto projects entering mass production. For the PMIC 
business, management expect growth to be flattish QoQ. 
2Q26 revenue beat GS and Street 
2Q26 revenue was NT$152.2bn (2% QoQ/1.2% YoY), above the company’s guidance of 
NT$140.2-$149.2bn, and 5.6%/3.7% higher vs. GSe/cons. 2Q26 GM of 46.2% was

Forecast changes 
We revise up our 2026E-28E earnings by 7%/2%/1% to reflect 1) stronger 2Q26

We increase our 12m TP to NT$7,000 from NT$6,800 previously, following our upward 
earnings revisions. Our TP is based on a target P/E multiple of 25x (unchanged; +1.8 stdv 
above 5 yr avg. fwd P/E) applied to our FY2H27E-1H28E EPS (unchanged). Overall, our 
12m TP implies ~97% upside; we reiterate our Buy rating on MediaTek.

### Page 7

Investment Thesis, Price Target Risks and Methodology

Investment Thesis - MediaTek 
MediaTek is a leading global IC design house specializing in smartphone AP (application 
processor). We have a positive stance on MediaTek, viewing it as well-positioned to 
transition from a traditional smartphone application provider to an AI-focused vendor, 
beginning with AI smartphones and extending to enterprise ASICs and smart automotive 
solutions (in partnership with NVIDIA) in 2025 and beyond. We expect MediaTek to 
achieve solid multi-year growth, where we expect revenue and earnings to increase by 
43%/59% CAGRs, respectively, in 2025-28E. This growth will be primarily driven by: 1) 
market share gains, particularly in the premium segment (specifically high-end 5G 
flagship SoCs), 2) strong ramp in AI ASIC business, and 3) new TAM in the 
automotive/computing sectors. 
Price Target Risks and Methodology - MediaTek (2454.TW) 
Valuation: Our 12m TP of NT$7,000 is based on a target P/E multiple of 25x (1.8 stdv 
above its 5-year trading average) applied to our 2H27E-1H28E EPS. 
Key risks to our views: (1) Weaker-than-expected end demand especially with 
smartphones, (2) Higher foundry cost impacting its margin outlook, (3) Intensifying 
competition that would result in reduced profitability, and (4) Slower ramp in ASIC would 
result in changes in operating leverage

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

M
Update
MediaTek  | Asia Pacific
Raising  2027 ASIC revenue and
more confident about 2028;
reiterate  OW
Increase in 2027 TPU revenue forecast should still be an upside surprise: The
company now expects it can gain 15%-20% revenue share in 2027 ASIC TAM of US
$80bn (excluding HBM cost). That implies US$12bn-US$16bn revenue, above its
prior outlook for US$7bn-US$12bn. We project 2027 TPU revenue at US$15.7bn.
Because TPU is the major ASIC project in global TAM, MediaTek’s implied TPU

market share could be 20% in 2027, similar to our preview.
Confident in 2nm TPU adopting EMIB-T packaging in 2028: The company is
confident that EMIB-T substrate yield can improve. We asked whether this project
still needs TSMC’s CoWoS capacity as a back-up, and management replied that
“switch to a second source would take time,” which to us means no need for now.
MediaTek also believes its ASIC revenue market share would further increase in
2028—very bullish, in our view.

3Q26 revenue guided to be flat to up 5% Q/Q, better than our down 5% Q/Q: We
cite a sustainable high-end smartphone segment and MediaTek’s chip price hike.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
MediaTek (2454.TW)
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions: cost of equity 9.2%, intermediate growth
rate 12.0%, terminal growth rate 3.0%.
Risks to Upside
n Edge AI proliferates smartphone replacement cycle.
n Smartphone demand rises in China and other EMs.
n New products attract high demand, resulting in market share gains.
n Google's TPU spurs demand.
Risks to Downside

n Smartphone demand deteriorates in China and other EMs.
n Competition heats up, resulting in pricing competition.
n New products attract low demand, causing market share loss.
n Margin dilution is more severe.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views

below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

the next few years. Although UMC’s AI revenue contribution remains modest today, management highlighted

and higher rack power density. As AI servers migrate toward increasingly power-intensive rack architectures, Delta
remains one of the key beneficiaries of higher system-level power content.

outlook while highlighting continued expansion across edge AI, automotive and enterprise AI opportunities. The
company has updated its AI ASIC revenue to US$12-16bn (based on 15-20% market share of its US$80bn market

estimate; lift from 10-15% market share; vs our estimate of US$18bn). Mediatek also confirmed that they are
working with second ASIC customer. GUC also continues to benefit from expanding hyperscaler ASIC CPU programs,
with industry discussions pointing to an increasingly robust multi-year design pipeline as cloud providers diversify
compute architectures beyond merchant GPUs, we expect CPU business to doubling next year with revenue
contribution to reach +30%/50% in 2026/27.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

## Document 135: 260803_gs_kinsus

Source PDF: all report pdf/260803_gs_kinsus.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-08-03
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Key Data  _____________________________________

GS Forecast  __________________________________

GS Factor Profile  ______________________________

We hosted Kinsus’ 2Q26 post-result follow-up call today (Aug 
3rd). Key takeaways include: 
(1) The company didn’t rule out the possibility of a potential supply 
shortage for ABF substrate in 2027/28 (previously mentioned that 
high layer count ABF substrate market to be in SD balance while 
expecting low layer count ABF substrate market to be in oversupply) 
as the company is receiving strong forecasts for next-gen AI related 
products with packaging size and layer counts that continue to go 
up. 
(2) In terms of pricing, Kinsus expect ABF and BT substrate pricing to 
have gone up by ~8% QoQ on average in 3Q/4Q26 with ABF 
substrate pricing to increase faster than BT substrate (reversing its 
previous expectation that BT substrate pricing to increase faster 
than ABF substrate in 2H26). The company expects revenue to see 
+10% QoQ in both 3Q/4Q26 (vs. 13% QoQ in 2Q26) and expects 
GM to reach 27-28/29% in 3Q/4Q26 (vs. 26% in 2Q26). 
(3) Without considering the potential pricing hike on ABF substrate 
going into 2027/28, Kinsus expects revenue in 2027/28 to increase 
at least 30% YoY per year while expecting GM to improve by 5ppt 
each year in 2027/28 as the company will continue to focus on 
product mix improvement and reaching higher UTR.  
(4) Kinsus announced a project CAPEX of NT$19.6bn, which will be 
split into 3 years and result in upward revision in 2026/27 CAPEX of 
~NT$14bn/~NT$16bn (75%+/60%+ upward revision from 
NT$8bn/NT$10bn). The project CAPEX is driven entirely by new 
facility construction while equipment is sponsored by key customers 
(3-4 customers or more). Moreover, Kinsus is considering to expand 
K7 and K8 Plant in Taiwan as customers seek to secure capacity 
beyond 2030, while capacity and investment scale have yet to be 
finalized.

More favorable ABF substrate supply demand outlook with potential supply shortage 
in 2027/28; Buy

### Page 3

(5) For AI-related products, Kinsus is targeting 100% market share for Vera CPU
substrate (previously targeting 50-100% market share for AI CPU substrate), expanding
from the current 50% share for Grace CPU substrate. The company continues to expect
its AI GPU market share to go up to 20% in coming quarters (vs. 10% now). Moreover,
the company also targets to gain market share in AMD/META/AWS’s AI accelerator
substrates in 2H26 and expects to further expand its share. The company expects AI

contribution to reach ~10% of total revenue in 2026, and expand to 15-20% in 2027.
(6) In terms of BT substrate demand, the company mentioned optical modules as being
one of the key drivers for the first time, given the PCB for optical modules applies a
similar production process to BT substrate (mSAP solutions), and expecting this segment
to reach 5% of total revenue in 2027 from <1% in 2026. Another driver for the BT
substrate business included demand from memory that will continue to grow in coming

years.
(7) In terms of BT substrate supply, the company hinted that it may potentially expand
capacity in China in 2027 to capture spillover orders from other ABF substrate suppliers,
as peers continue to convert BT substrate capacity to ABF substrate production (see
here).
(8) For future product opportunities, Kinsus is currently developing both glass/ceramic
core substrate, it is still at a very early stage of R&D and expects no/very limited revenue

contribution from this product prior to 2029.
(9) Kinsus reiteratedcits goal of reaching 80mn units of ABF capacity per month by the
end of 2029 (doubling capacity vs. now), with total facility construction spending
expected to be NT$45bn while the company mentioned that key customers will spend
NT$60-80bn for the equipment (with NT$60bn already secured) throughout 2026-29.
These customers will own 30mn units of the 2029 end capacity (~38% of total capacity)

due to its direct equipment investment.
Investment view
We are positive on Kinsus’ 2Q result (the solid jump on GM in 2Q -see here), and expect
the company to further deliver a strong revenue/GM expansion in 2H26 thanks to the
much better than expected market share on the high volume NVDA Vera CPU substrate
(the company expects 100% market share for Vera CPU substrate, while the company
only expected 50%+ three months ago – see here), which should contribute ~10%/15%

of the company’s ABF revenue or 15/20%+ of the ABF profit contribution in 2026/27E
with a much higher than company’s average GM/OPM.
Moreover, Kinsus’ tone on ABF substrates S/D outlook turns much more favorable (and
echos our view on more tightness on ABF substrates in coming years -see here), while
the company sees more and more high-layer/large size projects have been introduced
by customers who are asking for more capacity from Kinsus to support the demand,

despite the company having no numerical data to support this assumption, and still
input any like for like base pricing hike assumption in their +30% YoY revenue guidance
for 2027/28 or +5ppt GM YoY growth in 2027 &2028.
Going into LT, the company’s (1) new CAPEX raise (see here), (2) its new comment on
potential new K7/8 plant for 2030 and beyond new capacity expansion, and (3)
customers’ direct investment in ABF substrate capacity for more than NT$80bn in the

3 August 2026   3
Goldman Sachs Kinsus (3189.TW)For the exclusive use of KEVINLU@LENOVO.COM

### Page 4

next 3 years, should imply a long term demand sustainability and could improve investor 
confidence levels on long term AI demand sustainability. 
Overall, we maintain our Buy rating on Kinsus, and we expect the better market share in 
AI server CPU and its better position in the high-end ABF substrate industry into long 
term. We revise up our Kinsus TP to NT$1,125 (from NT$1,120). 
Earnings revisions 
We revise up our 2027/28/29E earnings estimate by 5/10/10% to factor in the solid 
GM/OPM expansion from a more favorable pricing outlook given the potential supply 
shortage environment (revise up 2027/28/29 GM by 0.9/1.7/1.2ppt and OPM by 
0.9/1.7/1.2ppt). 
We revise down our 2026E earnings estimate by 5% after reflecting a more conservative 
margin assumption on its IC substrate business in 2H26E, though we expect solid 
GM/OPM expansion in coming quarters.

Valuation 
We maintain our Buy rating with a new 12m TP of NT$1,125 (from NT$1,120), based on 
16.5x 2028E P/E, which is +1.2x STDV higher than Kinsus’ past upcycle average P/E 
(14.2x), and factor in our latest earnings revisions.

### Page 5

Investment Thesis - Kinsus
Kinsus is one of the key IC substrates suppliers globally with ~10% ABF/BT substrates
market share in 2024, with ~18% of total 2025 revenue contributed by its contact lens
subsidiary, Pegavision (6941.TW, Not Covered). With our view that the ABF substrate
shortage started from 2Q26 (ABF substrate return to supply demand balance from late
2025), we believe Kinsus’ performance will continue to improve compared with high-end

ABF peers considering the company’s increasing market share in the high-end ABF
substrate market; however, ~36% of the company’s revenue was contributed by BT
substrates in 2025 and 30% of the company’s ABF revenue is from non-LTA customers,
which we believe will continue to enjoy a better pricing outlook. As a result, we believe
Kinsus’ earnings outlook should continue to be strong (~106% 2026-29E CAGR), mainly
driven by better BT substrate and non-LTA ABF substrate pricing outlook, despite the

unfavorable demand outlook before 2H26. We believe Kinsus is an experienced
substrate supplier with a clear capacity expansion plan that can also upgrade its product
portfolio in the long term. We believe we are at the beginning of an industry upcycle,
making us positive on Kinsus’ stock performance over the next 12 months.
Price Target Risks and Methodology - Kinsus
Valuation methodology: Our 12-month target price of NT$1,125 for Kinsus is based on

16.5x 2028E P/E, which is +1.2x STDV higher than Kinsus’ past upcycle average P/E
(14.2x).
Key downside risks: (1) Slower-than-expected PC demand recovery, (2)
Slower-than-expected ABF substrates pricing upgrading outlook, (3)
Slower/Less-than-expected AI server orders from 2H26.

on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an

and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

營 運 分 析 
垂直整合 InP 磊晶至雷射晶片，台灣的 CW Laser 主力供應商 
聯亞光電成立於 1997 年,為台灣第一家專業光纖通信用磊晶片製造廠。公司
專注於以砷化鎵(GaAs)與磷化銦(InP)為基板的Ⅲ-Ⅴ族化合物半導體磊晶片
研發與製造,核心技術為有機金屬汽相磊晶(MOCVD),產品為雷射二極體 (LD) 
與檢光器 (PD) 磊晶片,波長涵蓋 650 至 1650nm,廣泛應用於光通訊、資料
中心、消費性光電、工業用雷射與醫療感測等領域。 
聯亞採 Epitaxy House 營運模式,向基板供應商採購 InP 與 GaAs 基板後,
於內部完成 MOCVD 磊晶與部分晶圓前段製程,再出貨予下游後段廠商進行
晶片製程、測試與封裝,最終產品進入光模組廠、系統廠與 CSP 終端客戶。
全球能量產光通訊用雷射磊晶片的廠商不足十家 ,聯亞為其中少數專注於光
通訊與資料中心應用的專業磊晶廠。 
公司產品應用分為三大區塊。資料通訊  (Datacom) 為目前營收絕對主力，
占比 80-85%，其中絕大多數為用於矽光模組之 CW Laser，終端客戶以美
國超大規模雲端服務業者 (Hyperscale CSP) 為主，並透過下游模組廠代工
出貨。電信 (Telecom) 相關產品占營收  5-10%，涵蓋光纖到府 (FTTH)、
長距離 DFB 雷射等傳統電信光源。其他產品約 5%，包括手機應用之雷射
光電二極體 (LDPD)、可見光通訊 (LiFi) 等仍在開發驗證中之新興應用。 
圖一、聯亞(3081) 2026Q1產品別營收比重

資料來源：聯亞，兆豐投顧彙整

- 3 - 
個股報告 
5、6月自結獲利續創高，Q2營運動能優於 Q1 
聯亞 5月自結稅後淨利 1.52 億元（YoY +407%），EPS 1.64元；6月自結稅
後淨利 1.71 億元（YoY +533%），EPS 1.68 元，單月獲利連續成長。5、6
月合計稅後淨利 3.23 億元，已超越 26Q1 單季稅後淨利 3.18 億元，顯示矽
光 CW Laser 出貨放量、產品組合改善及產能利用率提升持續反映於獲利。

公司 26Q2 營收 12.21億元（QoQ +35%／YoY +121%） ，延續逐月成長趨
勢；上半年營收21.25 億元（YoY +111%） 。隨矽光產品出貨增加、三吋基板
提升單片晶圓產出效率，以及後段合作廠商產能同步擴充， Q2 營收規模及
獲利均較 Q1進一步成長。

研究部預估 26Q2 毛利率 55.4%、營業利益率 44.4%，稅後淨利 4.47 億元，
EPS 4.39元。由於 5、6月公告 EPS係按股票股利發放前股數計算，若依 1
元股票股利追溯調整，兩月合計 EPS 約 3.02 元；與研究部 Q2 預估採相同
股本基準後，4月隱含 EPS 約 1.37 元，整體預估與已公布自結獲利相符。

下半年隨 1.6T 產品持續放量、3.2T 及多款新規格產品逐步進入量產，預估
26Q3／26Q4 營收分別為 14.31／15.45 億元。研究部將2026 年營收預估由
47.3 億元上修至 51.0 億元，毛利率由54.6%上修至 55.3%，反映客戶拉貨、
新規格產品出貨及營業槓桿皆優於前次預期。

51.2T交換器進入放量期，1.6T光模組需求自下半年加速 
AI 資料中心交換器持續由 25.6T 升級至 51.2T，帶動單埠速率由 400G／
800G 進一步轉向 1.6T。根據 650 Group 預估，全球 51.2T 交換器出貨將由
2024 年的約 7.7萬台增至 2028 年的 180 萬台，年複合成長率約 120%；依
此增速概算，2026／2027年出貨規模約 37／82萬台，顯示51.2T 平台將於
未來數季進入快速放量階段。

單台 51.2T 交換器可配置 64 個 800G 連接埠，或 32 個 1.6T 連接埠；若以
1.6T 配置概算，2026／2027 年交換器出貨可分別對應約 1,200／2,600 萬個
1.6T 連接埠需求，但實際光模組用量仍取決於交換器配置、網路拓撲及連接
埠使用率。2026 年資料中心交換器銷售額預估 YoY +86%，其中 Ethernet
交換器未來五年年複合成長率約 32%，亦支持AI叢集網路建置維持高成長。

聯亞 1.6T 矽光 CW Laser 已於 26Q1 量產，3.2T 產品按照時程也將進入量
產。隨 51.2T 交換器於下半年及 2027 年放量，1.6T 可插拔光模組需求將同
步提升；同時研究部現階段預估仍以可插拔光模組為主，尚未納入 CPO 大
規模量產貢獻。

- 4 - 
個股報告 
30億元 CB支應後續擴產，提前因應 2027－2028年需求 
聯亞於 2026年 6月 17日董事會決議發行國內第一次有擔保及第二次無擔保
轉換公司債，發行上限分別為 12 億元及 18 億元，合計募資上限 30 億元，
發行期間均為 3 年、票面利率 0%，募得資金主要用於購置機器設備等資本
支出。兩檔轉換公司債申報案件已於 7月 22 日生效。

此次募資將支應 MOCVD及晶圓前段設備擴充，以因應 2027－2028 年客戶
需求。依公司擴產規劃，MOCVD產能預計至 2027 年底較 2026 年底增加逾
100%。新購設備亦預留未來較大尺寸基板製程能力，曝光及晶圓前段設備升
級則有助提升單機產出與製程能力。

由於兩檔 CB 的最終轉換價格尚未公告，目前尚無法準確計算完全轉換後新
增股數。研究部 2026－2027 年 EPS 預估已反映 1 元股票股利使股數由約
9,252 萬股增加至約 1.018 億股，但尚未納入 CB 潛在轉換稀釋，後續將依
實際發行金額、轉換價格及轉換時點調整完全稀釋後 EPS。 
圖二、聯亞(3081)各季營收及獲利走勢預估

資料來源：Cmoney；兆豐投顧預估

營收高速成長帶動營業利益率擴張，2027年 EPS上看 36.37元 
研究部預估聯亞 2026 年營收 51.02 億元（YoY +131.6%），毛利率 55.3%
（YoY +12.6ppt） ， 營業利益22.82 億元 ， 營業利益率44.7%， 稅後淨利18.72
億元（YoY +337.1%），EPS 18.40 元。獲利增幅高於營收，主要反映矽光
CW Laser 出貨放量、高單價新規格產品比重提升，以及營收規模擴大後固
定製造費用與研發、管理費用的吸收效率改善。

- 5 - 
個股報告 
2027 年隨 1.6T 出貨規模持續擴大、3.2T 及新規格產品貢獻增加，以及新增
產能逐步開出，預估營收 91.60 億元（YoY +79.6%），毛利率56.4%（YoY 
+1.1ppt），營業利益45.79 億元，營業利益率 50.0%，稅後淨利 37.02 億元
（YoY +97.7%），EPS 36.37 元。毛利率僅小幅提升，主要來自產品組合
升級、產能利用率維持高檔及固定製造費用吸收改善；營業利益率進一步擴
張，則主要反映營收增幅高於研發及管理費用增幅，預估營業費用率由2025
年的 19.6%降至 2026 年的 10.6%，2027 年再降至 6.4%。

若將前次 EPS 依 1 元股票股利追溯調整，2026／2027 年 EPS 由 15.46／
29.35 元上修至 18.40／36.37元，分別上修約 19%／24%。 
圖三、聯亞(3081) P/E Band

資料來源：CMoney；兆豐投顧預估 
聯亞具備 CW Laser上游磊晶及晶圓前段製程能力 ， 受惠1.6T持續放量 、3.2T
及高單價新規格產品接續貢獻，2026／2027 年營收與 EPS 仍維持高速成
長，且毛利率逾 55%、營業利益率預估進一步升至 44.7%／50.0%，獲利能
力優於多數 AI光通訊相關公司，故仍給予高於同業的評價溢價。

考量近期 AI光通訊類股評價修正，加上公司進入大額資本支出階段，後續仍
有設備開出、良率爬坡及 CB 潛在股本稀釋等不確定性，研究部將目標價由
前次 3,713 元下修至為 2,325元(64 x 2027(F)PER)。儘管目標價下修，主因
為評價倍數調整，並非獲利預估下修；現價 1,630 元對應潛在上漲空間
42.6%，投資評等由「逢低買進」上調至「買進」。

個股報告

順達(3211) 
雖 Q2 營運逆風，然 BBU 需求強勁，全年展望不變 
投資評等 目標價 
逢低買進 $360 
前次投資建議：無

報告日期：2026.07.30 研 究 員：邱岳霖

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因 
➢ BBU出貨遞延，Q2營運不如預期，EPS 1.4 元。 
➢ 高瓦數 BBU有望於 H2 開始出貨，全年展望不變，2026 年 EPS 9.8元。 
➢ HVDC與高壓 BBU 預計於 2027 量產，產品組合續朝高瓦數升級，EPS 18.0元，兆豐投顧
給予目標價 360 元(20X 27(F)PER)，潛在上漲空間 16.9%，投資評等為「逢低買進」。

基本資料 結論與建議 
2026/7/29 收盤價： 308.00 ➢ 順達 (3211)公告 26Q2財報：營收 34.9億元， QoQ 
+4.4%/YoY +7.5% ，低於市場共識，主要受到 BBU客
戶拉貨遞延影響所致，在整體產品組合轉差下，毛利率
下滑至13.9%，季減6.1ppt，故稅後淨利2.07億元，QoQ 
-33.8%/YoY -65.1%，EPS 1.4元，低於市場預期。 
➢ 展望2026年，雖Q2客戶拉貨遞延帶動營運短期逆風，
不過客戶拉貨已恢復正常，同時隨AI資料中心備援電池
需求強勁且8KW、12KW高瓦數機種預計於 H2開始放
量，隨瓦數與電芯數增加帶動單價上揚，營運動能可望
回升，兆豐投顧預估順達2026年營收149.5億元，YoY 
+13.1%，在非IT（BBU）營收佔比可望超過整體營收
50%下，預估毛利率18.9%，年增2.3ppt，稅後淨利15.1
億元，YoY +9.2%，EPS 9.8元。 
➢ 展望 2027年，在電源架構升級下 BBU已然成為必需
品，且AI server供耗持續提升帶動更高壓與容量之BBU
需求持續增長，故順達持續於台灣與泰國擴充數倍產
能，同時公司亦持續投入研發25KW以上高瓦數BBU及
800V HVDC產品配合客戶開發送樣，並有望於2027年
開始量產，兆豐投顧預估順達2027年營收186.9億元，
YoY +25.0%，隨高瓦數產品比重提升，毛利率可望維
持在20%以上，整體獲利顯著擴大，預估稅後淨利27.8
億元，YoY +84.2%，EPS 18.0元，兆豐投顧給予目標
價360元(20X 27(F)PER)，潛在上漲空間16.9%，投資

- 2 - 
個股報告 
資料來源：CMoney；兆豐投顧預估

營 運 分 析

順達轉型加速，AI server BBU營收可望於 2026年佔比過半  
順達(3211)成立於 1998 年並於 2004 年掛牌上市，以筆記型電腦及行動裝置
鋰電池模組起家，公司於中國蘇州吳江、昆山及泰國大城皆有建立生產據點。
近年公司積極進行業務轉型，從傳統NB/平板電池模組跨入 AI伺服器備援電
池(BBU)、電動輔助自行車 (E-Mobility)及儲能系統 (ESS)等高毛利非 IT 應
用，目前產品涵蓋 NB/平板、伺服器 BBU、電動車用電池組及儲能模組，終
端應用遍及筆電品牌大廠、電源供應器大廠及 CSP資料中心客戶。順達原為
國內 NB 電池模組主力供應商，目前已成功將營運重心轉向 AI 伺服器 BBU
領域，非IT(以 BBU為主)營收佔比可望於 2026 年首度超車傳統 IT 業務，躍
升為公司最大營收貢獻來源，2025~2026 年持續擴充 BBU產能並升級規格，
也規劃規劃更高功率 HVDC 架構產品，全面迎接 AI 資料中心機櫃功耗提升
所帶動的備援電力需求。

- 3 - 
個股報告

BBU 出貨遞延，Q2營運不如預期，EPS 1.4元 
順達(3211)公告 26Q2 財報：營收 34.9 億元，QoQ +4.4%/YoY +7.5%，低
於市場共識，主要受到 BBU客戶拉貨遞延影響所致，在整體產品組合轉差
下，毛利率下滑至 13.9%，季減 6.1ppt，故稅後淨利 2.07 億元，QoQ 
-33.8%/YoY -65.1%，EPS 1.4元，低於市場預期。

BBU 拉貨重啟，非 IT 營收比重續增，獲利動能回升，EPS 2.8元 
兆豐投顧預估順達 26Q3 營收 38億元，QoQ +8.9%/YoY +5.0%，主要受惠
BBU機種客戶自 7月恢復正常拉貨，同時 AI 資料中心備援電池需求強勁，
預期非 IT 營收佔比持續提升，帶動整體營收回升；在產品組合改善下，毛利
率回升至 20.0%，季增 6.1ppt，稅後淨利 4.4 億元，QoQ +111.9%/YoY 
+83.9%，EPS 2.8元。

高瓦數 BBU有望於 H2開始出貨，全年展望不變，2026年 EPS 9.8元 
展望 2026 年，雖 Q2客戶拉貨遞延帶動營運短期逆風，不過客戶拉貨已恢復
正常，同時隨 AI資料中心備援電池需求強勁且 8KW、12KW高瓦數機種預
計於 H2開始放量，隨瓦數與電芯數增加帶動單價上揚，營運動能可望回升，
兆豐投顧預估順達 2026 年營收 149.5 億元，YoY +13.1%，在非 IT（BBU）
營收佔比可望超過整體營收 50%下，預估毛利率 18.9%，年增 2.3ppt，稅後
淨利 15.1 億元，YoY +9.2%，EPS 9.8元。

HVDC與高壓 BBU 預計於 2027量產，產品組合續朝高瓦數升級，EPS 18.0
元 
展望 2027 年，在電源架構升級下 BBU已然成為必需品，且 AI server 供耗
持續提升帶動更高壓與容量之 BBU需求持續增長，故順達持續於台灣與泰國
擴充數倍產能，同時公司亦持續投入研發 25KW以上高瓦數 BBU 及 800V 
HVDC產品配合客戶開發送樣，並有望於 2027 年開始量產，兆豐投顧預估
順達 2027 年營收 186.9 億元，YoY +25.0%，隨高瓦數產品比重提升，毛利
率可望維持在 20%以上，整體獲利顯著擴大，預估稅後淨利27.8 億元，YoY 
+84.2%，EPS 18.0元。

期未來六個月股價跌幅超過-30%）。

紀彥呈 / 資訊軟體、半導體  
+886-2-2326-9899#1940 / ianchi@cathayfut.com.tw

聯電 2303 TT  ( 電子–半導體 ) 
稼動攀升，卓越非凡 
投資與評價 
1. 2Q26 聯電 22/28 奈米營收占比達 37%，整體產能利用率攀升至 85%，
營運復甦加速；3Q26 將進行價格調整 ， 且新加坡 Fab 12i 第三期產能開
始提高營收貢獻，稼動率再度上升至 90%，帶動毛利率上揚。 
2. 聯電 26 年營運回溫，2H26 受惠 F12i 貢獻、稼動率回升、特殊製程放
量與漲價，帶動獲利上修，EPS 7.52 元；27 年 12nm 專案放量，開始
貢獻營收，且先進封裝、DTC、bridge die 與矽光子落地，EPS 8.13 元。 
3. 目前聯電股價交易在 102.5 元(12.6X 修正後 27(F) PER)， 位於近三年參
考本益比區間 9~22x 中間偏下位置，考量成熟製程復甦加快，稼動率攀
升，且矽光子將開始貢獻營收，維持買進投資建議，但因應需求開始擴
產，資本支出將大幅上升，預期未來將對毛利率有所稀釋，本益比由上
緣 20 倍調降至平均值 16 倍，目標價 130 元(16X 修正後 27(F) PER)。 
重點更新 
需求提升使聯電 2Q26 稼動率達 85%，業外挹注帶動獲利創近年新高 
成熟製程需求開始多點復甦，通訊領域受惠網通、顯示驅動 IC、影像訊號處
理器及部分 FPGA 需求回升，帶動 12 吋 22/28 奈米產能利用率提升，晶圓
出貨量季增 10.6%至 112.9 萬片 12 吋約當晶圓 ， 產能利用率由前一季之79%
提升至 85%，2Q26 營收 687.33 億元(+12.6% QoQ)，符合預期。受到稼動
率提升、固定成本吸收效率改善及 22/28 奈米產品占比提高帶動，毛利率
32.5%(+3.3ppts QoQ)。營業利益 149.50 億元(+39.5% QoQ)，符合預期。
本季另有投資利益與股息等營業外收益 302.36 億元挹注，推升稅後淨利至
422.24 億元(+162.0% QoQ)，EPS 3.39 元，優於預期。 
預期在需求提升下聯電 3Q26 產能利用率達九成，本業為近年新高 
新加坡 Fab 12i 第三期產能開始提高營收貢獻，小幅上修晶圓出貨量 1.7%，
整體產能利用率由 89.2%上調至 90.0%， 上調3Q26 營收預估 2.7%至 764.18
億元(+11.2% QoQ) 。受到稼動率提升、特殊製程占比增加及固定成本吸收
效率改善帶動 ，部分抵銷新加坡新增產能折舊壓力 ， 調升毛利率預估至36.4% 
(+3.9ppts QoQ)。因持續加大 TFLN 研發投入，調高營業費用至 90.88 億元
(+23.3% QoQ) ，上調營業利益至 187.58 億元(+25.5% QoQ) ，稅後淨利
171.04 億元(-59.5% QoQ)，EPS 1.37 元，本業為近年新高。

預期在需求提升下聯電 3Q26 產能利用率達九成，本業為近年新高 
新加坡 Fab 12i 第三期產能開始提高營收貢獻，8 吋需求受惠高壓製程、BCD、PMIC、MCU
與顯示驅動 IC 回溫，12 吋則由 22/28 奈米、影像訊號處理器、CIS、FPGA、interposer 及部
分通訊晶片需求帶動，應用涵蓋通訊、消費、車用、AI 伺服器與邊緣 AI 裝置，小幅上修晶圓
出貨量 1.7%，整體產能利用由 89.2%上調至 90.0%，調高 3Q26 營收預估 2.7%至 764.18 億
元(+11.2% QoQ )。受到產能利用提升、特殊製程占比增加及固定成本吸收效率改善帶動，並
部分抵銷新加坡新增產能折舊壓力 ，調升毛利率預估 1 個百分點至 36.4%， 高於2Q26 之 32.5%。
因持續加大先進封裝、DTC、bridge die 、矽光子、TFLN 及 12 奈米平台研發投入，調高營業
費用至 90.88 億元(+23.3% QoQ)，上調營業利益預估 6.8%至 187.58 億元(+25.5% QoQ)，因
2Q26 業外基期較高，稅後淨利 171.04 億元(-59.5% QoQ)，EPS 1.37 元，本業為近年新高。

From mature nodes to specialty platforms, UMC’s 2026 performance reaches a 
record high. 
聯電 26 年營運復甦動能來自成熟製程需求改善、特殊製程放量及 AI 相關業務成長，22/28 奈
米受惠手機 AMOLED 驅動 IC、網通、ISP 與 FPGA 需求，8 吋則由 MCU、PMIC、高壓製程、
BCD 及 RF-SOI 支撐 ， 搭配新加坡Fab 12i 產能逐步開出 ，AI 相關的電源管理 、 連接晶片 、FPGA、
矽光子及先進封裝營收亦開始增加， 3Q26 與 4Q26 稼動率分別由 89.2%/91.2%上修至
90.0%/91.3%，帶動固定成本吸收效率持續改善。價格端來看，預期聯電 3Q26 起啟動價格調
整，主要反映原物料、能源、物流與長期技術投資成本上升，有助抵銷新加坡F12i 折舊高峰對
毛利率的壓力。與 Intel 合作之 12nm 專案進展順利，預計 26 年提供 PDK 與 IP，應用涵蓋
Wi-Fi、數位電視與高速介面。先進封裝目前也已與超過 10 家客戶合作，26 年預計有超過 35
個新 tape-out 案件。 
上修 26 年營收預估 1.6%至 2,850.90 億元(+20.0% YoY)。為了因應 AI 資料中心、汽車電子及
新興應用帶來的中長期需求，將在新加坡及台南廠區進行擴產，26 年全年 CAPEX 預估由 15
億美元調高至 20 億美元 ，隨全年產能利用率持續回升 、22/28 奈米及特殊製程產品組合改善，
以及下半年價格環境轉佳，固定成本吸收效率提升，抵銷部分折舊費用增加的影響 ，上調毛利
率預估 0.4 個百分點至 34.0%(+5.0ppts YoY)。 因持續投入12 奈米 、 矽光子 、 先進封裝 、DTC，
調升營業費用至 322.87 億元(+19.8% YoY )，在營收成長與毛利率提升帶動下，調高營業利益
至 646.00 億元(+54.0% YoY)。 除本業獲利改善外 ，2Q26 認列的大額權益法投資利益亦推升全
年營業外獲利，上修稅後淨利預估 47.5%至 937.42 億元(+125.7% YoY )，EPS 7.52 元，營運
創歷史新高。

### Page 4

預期聯電 27 年擴產布局高毛利新業務，營運再創歷史新高 
聯電 27 年成長動能將延續自成熟製程供需改善、特殊製程滲透率提升及 AI 相關平台逐步商業
化，22/28 奈米、BCD、高壓製程、RF-SOI、PMIC 及低功耗邏輯製程持續受惠網通、電源管
理、車用、工控與邊緣 AI 需求；12 奈米合作案則進入客戶設計導入及設計定案階段，初期以
試產與小量爬坡為主，矽光子、DTC、bridge die 、interposer 及混合鍵合等方案亦隨客戶驗
證進展開始貢獻營收 。矽光子與光學相關平台具備成為高毛利新業務的潛力，主要因 PIC 與
TFLN 多採 65nm/55nm 及 8 吋製程，雖不需最先進邏輯節點，但需要穩定的特殊製程能力、
材料整合與高良率量產經驗，與聯電既有成熟製程優勢高度契合，有望由研發驗證進入小量放
量階段，成為 8 吋產能利用率與毛利率改善的補充動能，且為了反映化材、矽晶圓以及電價成
本提升，27 年可能全面與客戶協商價格，預估混合 ASP 調漲約 10%。 
在產能利用率提升與價格改善帶動下，調高 27 年營收預估 5.9%至 3,678.54 億元(+29.0% YoY)。
為了因應需求，聯電將擴建新加坡 P4 廠區無塵室，主要用於未來矽光子產能，既有 P3 廠區則
將陸續增加 BCD 電源管理及矽光子相關設備，預計在完成設備採購、裝機與認證後，於 27 年
底至 28 年初開始量產爬坡，也將在既有 8 吋廠建置 TFLN 鈮酸鋰薄膜相關產能 ， 以支援高速光
通訊應用。台南方面，聯電規劃興建 12A 廠區的 P7 及 P8 廠房，作為先進封裝業務的長期擴
充基礎，初期以建立廠房空間為主，未立即全面裝設無塵室及生產設備；既有 12A 廠則將先導
入客製化記憶體堆疊及深溝槽電容等方案 。 未來新增產能將涵蓋晶圓對晶圓堆疊 、 記憶體堆疊、
DTC 及其他異質整合技術，27 年資本支出由原先預估之 16 億美元大幅上調至 30 億美元，受
到 ASP、產能利用率同步改善，以及矽光子和先進封裝等差異化業務占比提升帶動抵銷折舊稀
釋影響，毛利率 41.2%(+7.2ppts YoY)。調升營業利益至 1,102.38 億元(+70.6% YoY)，上修稅
後淨利預估 6.6%至 1,014.53 億元(+8.2% YoY)，EPS 8.13 元，營運再創新高。

### Page 5

成熟製程需求改善，聯電稼動率維持高檔 
聯電短期受惠成熟製程需求改善，22nm、Wi-Fi、數位電視機上盒、MCU、LCD 控制器與電
源管理 IC 等應用補貨動能升溫，帶動 8 吋與 12 吋稼動率同步回升。中長期來看，公司將成長
重心由傳統成熟製程延伸至特殊製程與異質整合平台 ， 包含嵌入式高壓 、BCD、RF-SOI、eNVM、
12nm、bridge die 、DTC、interposer 與矽光子解決方案，並透過 PDK 1.0 支援切入 PIC、
OIO、OCS 與 CPO 等應用，預計 27 年開始逐步發酵。 
目前聯電股價交易在 102.5 元(12.6X 修正後 27(F) PER) ，位於近三年參考本益比區間 9~22x
中間偏下位置，考量成熟製程復甦加快，稼動率攀升，且先進封裝與矽光子將開始貢獻營收，
維持買進投資建議，但為了因應需求而開始擴產，26、27 年資本支出皆大幅上升，預期未來將
對毛利率有所稀釋，本益比由區間上緣 20 倍調降至平均值 16 倍，目標價 130 元(16X 修正後
27(F) PER)。

### Page 6

公司簡介 
聯電是一家專注於純晶圓代工的半導體公司 ， 其核心競爭力並不在於先進製程節點的技術領先 ，
而是在成熟製程的量產能力、良率穩定度、成本控制與長期客戶關係。公司長期深耕 28 奈米
以上製程，並在顯示驅動 IC、影像感測、嵌入式非揮發記憶體、高壓與功率元件等特色製程平
台建立規模優勢。從產業定位來看，聯電更接近景氣循環型但具結構性支撐的公司，而非高度
技術競賽導向的成長型公司。

在獲利結構上，聯電的關鍵驅動因子主要來自稼動率與產品組合的變化。成熟製程的固定成本
比重高，因此當整體需求回溫、產能利用率上升時，毛利率通常會呈現明顯改善。相反地，若
需求疲弱導致稼動率下滑，即使營收跌幅有限，獲利仍可能受到放大衝擊。此外，營收成長的
品質也高度依賴產品組合，來自特色製程或車用、工控平台的訂單，對平均售價與毛利率的貢
獻，明顯優於一般消費性成熟製程產品。

成熟製程市場本身具有較強的商品化特性，價格與供需循環對聯電的短中期表現影響甚大。相
較先進製程由少數廠商主導，成熟製程更容易受到同業擴產、價格競爭以及客戶庫存調整的影
響。因此，在研究聯電時，不能僅以公司自身營運為出發點，而必須同時評估整體成熟製程產
能是否出現結構性過剩，以及終端市場是否進入補庫存或去庫存階段。

圖 2. Revenue by Application

資料來源: UMC、國泰證期研究部整理

在中長期結構上，車用與工業應用被視為聯電相對穩定的成長支柱。這類應用具備產品生命週
期長 、 轉換成本高與品質要求嚴格等特性 ， 有助於降低價格波動與需求急劇反轉的風險 。 然而，
其導入與認證週期較長，對短期財務數字的拉動效果有限，因此更適合作為評估聯電估值下緣
與營運穩定度的依據，而非短線成長動能。

### Page 7

聯電 ESG 政策 
聯華電子股份有限公司以創造以人為本、與環境共生、與社會共榮的全球性友善生態新價值作
為永續發展的核心願景，將環境(Environmental)、社會(Social)與公司治理(Governance)全面
整合至公司營運策略與決策架構之中 。 聯電認為 ， 企業長期競爭力不僅來自於技術與製造能力，
更奠基於穩健治理、負責任的環境管理，以及對員工、供應鏈與社會的正向影響。基於此，聯
電持續透過制度化的 ESG 政策與行動方案，確保公司在追求營運成長的同時，能有效降低對
環境與社會所造成的衝擊，並回應利害關係人之期待。

1. 公司治理： 
在公司治理面向，聯電以董事會作為  ESG 治理之最高層級，負責督導永續策略方向與重大決
策。董事會下設之永續發展暨提名委員會由全體獨立董事組成，專責監督  ESG 治理成效，而 
ESG 指導委員會則由共同總經理擔任主席，結合多位核心高階主管，負責永續策略目標的訂定
與執行。透過此一由上而下的治理體系，聯電確保永續議題能夠實質納入公司策略、資本配置
與績效管理之中，並定期向董事會報告執行成果，使  ESG 不僅停留於政策宣示，而是成為公
司治理的一部分。 
圖 3. ESG 治理組織架構圖

資料來源:UMC、國泰證期研究部整理

2. 風險治理與誠信經營： 
在風險治理與誠信經營方面，聯電將  ESG 議題全面納入企業風險管理架構，依循  COSO 企
業風險管理原則，系統性辨識、評估並管理營運過程中可能產生的治理、環境與社會風險。企
業風險管理委員會負責跨部門整合風險資訊，並與  ESG 指導委員會保持密切連結，確保氣候
變遷、供應鏈、人權、資訊安全等重大永續風險，能即時反映於公司決策與因應策略之中。此
一治理設計，使聯電能在高度變動的產業環境中，維持營運穩健與長期韌性。 
圖 4. 企業風險管理組織架構圖

資料來源:UMC、國泰證期研究部整理

### Page 8

3. 環境政策： 
在環境政策方面，聯電承諾以低碳轉型與資源效率為核心，朝 2050 年淨零排放目標邁進，並
積極響應 RE100 再生能源倡議。公司持續推動氣候行動，透過製程改善、設備升級與能源結
構轉型，有效降低溫室氣體排放。至 2024 年，聯電範疇一與範疇二溫室氣體排放量已較基準
年減量 31%， 顯示公司正穩健地行走於既定的減碳路徑上 。 同時 ， 聯電亦導入內部碳定價機制，
將每噸二氧化碳當量的成本納入投資與管理決策，藉此驅動更多具實質效益的減碳行動。

圖 5. 環境目標與績效表

資料來源:UMC、國泰證期研究部整理

4. 水資源管理： 
除氣候行動外，聯電亦重視水資源管理、廢棄物減量與生物多樣性保護。公司透過提升再生水
與海淡水使用比例，降低對天然水資源的依賴，同時推動廢棄物資源化與循環經濟模式，持續
減少單位產品的環境足跡。在污染防制方面，聯電針對空氣污染與水污染設有嚴格管理機制，
確保排放表現優於法規標準，並逐步將自然相關風險與機會納入長期環境管理規劃。

5. 社會政策 
在社會政策方面，聯電以人權保障與人才發展為核心，致力打造安全、健康、多元與共融的職
場環境。公司遵循國際人權準則與責任商業聯盟行為守則，確保員工在招聘、任用、升遷與薪
酬制度中受到公平對待，並透過完善的職業安全與健康管理體系，持續降低職災風險。在人才
培育與留任方面，聯電透過教育訓練、職涯發展與員工持股等制度，強化員工向心力與長期承
諾，使人力資本成為支持公司永續成長的重要基礎。

2Q26 業外貢獻、獲利優於預期，3Q26 產能利用率提升至 90% 
2Q26 營收表現略優於預期約 3%，業外收益主要來自投資收益 300.5 億元，
調整後稅後 EPS為 3.36元，其中約 2.2元來自業外、占 65%，累計 1H26
業外貢獻 EPS約 2.58元。3Q26產能季增約 1.5%至 132.5萬片，整體產能
利用率再提升至 90%以上，帶動晶圓出貨季增 5~10%，ASP持平、並未如
市場所預期的漲價 5~10%，毛利率則因產能利用率提升將成長至約 35%。
將 3Q26 營收上修 3.8%至 763.4 億元，季增 11.1%、年增 29.1%，稅後
EPS預估為 1.72元、上修 39.3%。 
上修資本支出擴產，12 奈米 FinFET 貢獻需待 2028 年 
2026年資本支出由 15億美元上修至 20億美元，且董事會已通過至 2028年
總計約 50億美元的可運用資金，將用在 1) 擴建新加坡廠 P4無塵室以提供
PIC產能；2) 台南 P7、P8新建廠房，預期 2H28~2029年量產，屆時整體
月產能將增加 2~2.5萬片，因資本支出增加，預估 2026/2027年折舊皆將年
增 10~15%。與 Intel合作的 12奈米 FinFET PDK 需待 2026年底準備就緒、
2027年客戶開始導入，原預期 2H27即可開始貢獻營收，但目前營收貢獻的
時間點將延後至 2028年，預估屆時將貢獻約 8%營收。 
2027 年本業獲利年增約 40%，目前評價偏低、調升至買進 
成熟製程市場需求狀況改善，儘管消費性電子需求仍疲弱，但下游客戶庫存
水位並未明顯升高，聯電產能利用率提升及產品組合改善、加上新加坡廠及
台南廠開始進行擴產計畫，整體營運已開始復甦。預估 2026/2027 年稅後
EPS 分別為 8.15 元/7.77 元，扣除 1H26 業外投資收益後， 2026 年稅後
EPS為 5.57元，2027年營收年增 17.1%、本業獲利將年增 34.1%，營運重
回成長軌道，近期股價修正，以 2027年獲利計算目前 PE評價約 13倍已降
至偏低位置，故調升評等至買進，目標價 125元(16X 2027F EPS)。 
元大觀點 
⧫  
2Q26 獲利 65%來自業外投資收益，1H26 累計業外貢獻 EPS 約 2.58
元。3Q26 展望正向，產能利用率提升至 90%以上。 
⧫  
2026 年資本支出由 15 億美元上修至 20 億美元，擴建新加坡 PIC 無塵
室及台南新廠，台南新廠預計 2H28~2029 年量產。 
⧫  
2027 年本業獲利有望年增 40%，目前 PE 評價降至 13 倍偏低位置，
調升評等至買進，目標價 125 元(16X 2027F EPS)。

營運分析  
2Q26 獲利優於預期，其中業外貢獻約 65%獲利

增 16.8%至 112.9萬片(約當 12吋)，ASP季增約 1.6%，營收表現略優於預期約 3%，其中 28/22
奈米比重由 1Q26的 34%提升至 37%，產品組合改善加上產能利用率提升，毛利率 32.5%優於預
期、亦優於公司原財測的約 30%，業外收益貢獻約 302.4億元、主要來自投資收益 300.5億元，調
整後稅後 EPS為 3.36元，其中約 2.2元來自業外、占 65%，累計 1H26業外貢獻 EPS約 2.58元。

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：晶圓代工 2026年 7月 30日 UMC 第3 頁，共 14頁
3Q26 產能利用率提升至 90%，但報價未如市場預期上漲 5~10%
3Q26電腦、通訊、消費性電子需求穩健，8吋需求來自 PMIC、Sensor、MCU等應用，帶動整體
產能利用率再提升至 90%以上，其中 8吋由約 80%提升至 85%，12吋則仍維持在 90%以上，而
新加坡廠產能擴充、整體產能季增約 1.5%至 132.5萬片，帶動晶圓出貨季增 5~10%，ASP持平、
並未如市場所預期的漲價 5~10%，毛利率則因產能利用率提升將成長至約 35%。將 3Q26 營收上
修 3.8%至 763.4億元，季增 11.1%、年增 29.1%，稅後 EPS預估為 1.72元、上修 39.3%。

上修資本支出擴產，12 奈米 FinFET 貢獻需待 2028 年
AI相關的 PMIC、連接通訊等應用具成長動能，2026年營收貢獻近 3億美元、約占營收 3%，聯電
看好至 2030年有望達到 10億美元營收，因此將 2026年資本支出由 15億美元上修至 20億美元，
且聯電董事會已通過至 2028年總計約 50億美元的可運用資金，將用在 1) 擴建新加坡廠 P4無塵
室以提供 PIC產能；2) 台南 P7、P8新建廠房，台南新廠預計將耗時 20個月，預期 2H28~2029
年量產，屆時整體月產能將增加 2~2.5 萬片，因資本支出增加，預估 2026/2027 年折舊皆將年增
10~15%。
PIC業務主要提供 TFLN調變器給 400G/Lane的需求，且以 12吋生產良率優於同業的 8吋，目前
已交付首批產品，預期 2027年可較顯著貢獻營收。與 Intel合作的 12奈米 FinFET目前進展順利，

但 PDK需待 2026年底準備就緒、2027年客戶開始導入，原預期 2H27即可開始貢獻營收，但目
前營收貢獻的時間點將延後至 2028年，預估屆時將貢獻約 8%營收。

### Page 4

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：晶圓代工 2026年 7月 30日 UMC 第4 頁，共 14頁  
產業概況 
台灣晶圓代工產值年增 34%，成熟製程產業狀況改善 
台灣半導體產業持續受惠 AI需求發展，先進製程優先受惠，預估 2026年台灣晶圓代工產業產值年
增 35~40%，而台積電的市占率約 94%，營收成長仍優於其他晶圓代工同業，而台積電規劃將 6吋、
8吋成熟製程產能轉作利潤更高的先進封裝，預估 2027 年每月將有約 9萬片的產能釋出，約可提
升聯電、世界先進的成熟製程產能利用率 10~15%，其中世界的新加坡 12吋廠預計 2027年投產，
較有望承接來自台積電的轉單效益，預估可增加 1~1.5 元 EPS，受惠程度較大。而整體成熟製程
仍受惠 AI需求爆發所帶來的外溢效果，產業狀況持續改善。

### Page 5

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：晶圓代工 2026年 7月 30日 UMC 第5 頁，共 14頁  
獲利調整與股票評價  
2027 年本業獲利年增約 40%，目前評價偏低、調升至買進 
成熟製程市場需求狀況改善，儘管消費性電子需求仍疲弱，但下游客戶庫存水位並未明顯升高，而
2H26報價並未如市場預期上漲 5~10%，但聯電新產品規劃及市占提升，帶動產能利用率提升及產
品組合改善、有利於聯電的獲利表現，加上新加坡廠及台南廠開始進行擴產計畫，整體營運已開始
復甦。 
預估 2026/2027年稅後 EPS分別為 8.15元/7.77元，扣除 1H26業外投資收益後，2026年稅後
EPS為 5.57元，2027年營收年增 17.1%、本業獲利將年增 34.1%，營運重回成長軌道，近期股
價修正，以 2027年獲利計算目前 PE評價約 13倍已降至偏低位置，故調升評等至買進，目標價
125元(16X 2027F EPS)。

### Page 9

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：晶圓代工 2026年 7月 30日 UMC 第9 頁，共 14頁

公司簡介  
聯電設立於 1980年，主要業務為晶圓製造服務，其製程以 22nm以上的成熟製程為主。主要廠區分布於台灣、中國廈門、
日本以及新加坡。客戶包含聯發科、 Qualcomm、Samsung、NXP、德州儀器等國際大廠。公司發展策略不同於台積電，
其以晶圓製造服務為基礎，轉投資多家半導體晶片設計公司，並以自有產能及技術扶植半導體晶片設計公司，而當半導體
晶片設計公司之產品在市場中具競爭優勢取得需求量時，亦將回饋聯電，得以維持晶圓代工產能利用率，案例如聯陽、聯
詠、智原、原相、盛群等 IC設計及設計服務公司。
